Playtest rules to watch

These rules stand in for rules the full game will add. Tell us whether each one worked at your table.

  • The interim day and its care window at the start of a session played outside an Expedition and outside Downtime, with every soldier in scope and Field Repair allowed. Did gear and injuries carry over sensibly from one session to the next?
  • The interim issue and Funding 3. Did soldiers start each session or Expedition with too much gear, or too little?
  • The setup table. Which field ratings, zone mixes, and Titans came up, and did any combination feel unfair or dull?
  • Health. Soldiers now hold 4 to 8 boxes. How many Critical Injuries did a soldier carry before going Down, and did any fight feel safe because of it?
  • Retargeting and Frenzy. How often did a Titan turn onto a second soldier because its holder was out of reach, and what Frenzy was it at when it died? Did any fight run long enough to reach 3, and did the table feel the pressure to finish?
  • The Sprinting Abnormal. When a Squad screened it and cut at its legs, did it feel easier or harder than a standard Medium Titan, and when did it run at nobody?
  • The field and the Stride. Could the table find every zone, Stride, and Carry quickly on the printed field? Did the terrain change where the Squad chose to fight, did a wreck ever close a Blind Spot, and did a Titan's Stride ever surprise anyone?
  • Zone rules marked Playtest rule. A forced step toward Distant from a zone with no body, a one-step Flight off the field, and zone effects that harm no one. Did any of them come up, and did the result feel right?
  • The retreat clock. Did a retreat begin? Who got out, who was carried, who stayed beside a Pinned comrade, and who was left behind? Did the stay limit end a stay, and did any arm or leg burn away under a Corpse?
  • Expeditions. Which Formation Posts, Paces, and hazard rows came up? Did rations run short? Did the Straggler row or the Night table feel unfair or dull?
  • Downtime. Did the infirmary's seven days send soldiers out fit enough? Which Downtime Actions and Squad Action did the table choose, and was one Downtime Action each enough?
  • Requisition. What did players ask for, what did Command grant, and did the Ledger stop anyone? Did a Kept Item matter on the next Expedition?
  • Skirmishes. How did each end: Grit, a Parley, the Squad leaving, or no one standing? Did the foe rule ever pick a target the table did not expect? Did anyone make the Fear Roll for a first human kill?
  • Called rolls and Circumstances. How often did the GM call a roll, and did the stakes named first feel fair? Which steps came up, and did any roll feel priced twice?
  • Waypoint scenes. What did the GM frame, and did a scene slow the ride or lift it?
  • Directed Foes. Did the table hear and accept each reason? Did a group break sooner than its Grit?
  • Fear Rolls the GM called. How many, and how many Scars did they give?
  • A new character after a death. With no Squadmate to promote, how long did the player wait before their new character joined, and what did they do meanwhile?

Questions

  1. How many rounds did a prepared Squad need to kill a Medium Titan?
  2. When a Titan attacked, did rolling its Attack Dice in the open, announcing the Severity, and then dodging keep the card moving? Did anyone forget that one dodge covers all of that Titan's cards for the round?
  3. Did anyone use Break Attention with a Feint? Did it open a Nape strike before the Titan's next card?
  4. Did a soldier strike a Nape with no Openings or Help? What did they need, and did it land?
  5. Did a canister run dry or ODM Gear Jam during a fight? In which round?
  6. Did Steam or a Falling Titan catch anyone? Who Leapt Clear, who was Pinned, and how did each pin end: a Heave, cutting the pinning Body Part, a living Titan standing, or death under the body?
  7. Which two Squad Tactics did the Squad choose, and were they used?
  8. Did the Attention Ladder ever choose a soldier the table did not expect? Describe the Positions and marks.
  9. Did a second Focus Titan enter? Could the table follow every soldier's zone and attachment with both Titans on the field?
  10. What did you reach for and not have: a piece of gear, an action, or a rule?
  11. Was any rule read two ways at the table? Which, and how did you settle it?
  12. What was the most memorable moment of the session?

Notes
